Test 25 out of 36
 I2C DA 5V Port Pin Test        Next Test: I2C CLK 5V Port Pin Test
 Enter "C"ontinue to next instruction,"B"ack to go beginning of last Test, "S"kip to the next Test or "Q"uit

 Instructions:
  !!! This is where you might blow you're ESP32 if your not careful connecting to the correct pins  !!!! 
   - Move the wire jumper from J2 pin closest to the inside of board to the J21-5 pin closest to the inside of board
   - Move the wire jumper from J22 pin closest to the inside of the board to J21-5 pin closest to the outside of board.
   - The Serial Monitor will update when wire jumper is removed from ground pin and replaced on ground pin (pin 21-5 outside)
 Enter "C"ontinue to end this test.

   - then enter "C" 
   - The Serial Monitor should display the servo angle and the servo should swing forward then backwards.
       NOTE: Once you are finished with this test you will need to enter "C" to stop the scrolling data.

 Instructions:

Test 34 out of 36
 Serial RX0 pin test GPIO3       Next Test: Encoder/Motor Controller A Test
 Enter "C"ontinue to next instruction,"B"ack to go beginning of this Test, "S"kip Next Test or "Q"uit

 Instructions:
   - If test worked enter "C" 
   - If did not work, unplug the MSEduino from power sources (USB) and 
   --- Check that the wire jumper is connected correctly to three the pin header J16 pin closest inside of board
   --- Check that the other end is connected to the three pin header J3 pin closest to the inside of board
   --- Check the solder joints on the three pin header J3, GPIO3 (physical pin 34)
   --- Use a multimeter to check continuity (beep test), to ground, to 3V3, and to points along the circuit path (use the schematic and board artwork as reference)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


Test 35 out of 36
Test: Encoder/Motor Controller A Test    Next Test: Encoder/Motor Controller B Test
 Enter "C"ontinue to next instruction,"B"ack to go beginning of last Test, "S"kip to the next Test or "Q"uit

 Instructions:
   - Remove servo.
   - Plug Motor Controller Board into the two pin and 4 pins receptacle on the board (around the mounter hole).
   - Using one of the metal gear motors, plug the motor wires M1 and M2 into MOTOR_A header on Motor Control Board.
   - Plug the motor encoder wires into MSEduino board as per the following. Plug encoder wire C1 into header J35 top pin (closest to inside of board).
   - Plug the encoder VCC wire into the middle pin of J35 and encoder ground wire into the bottom (outside) pin J35.
   - Plug encoder C2 wire into top pin in J32 (closest to inside of board).
   - then enter "C" 
   - The Serial Monitor should display the Encode count and the motor should be spinning.
       NOTE: Once you are finished with this test you will need to enter "C" to stop the scrolling data.
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

   - If did not work, unplug the MSEduino from power sources (USB) and 
   --- Check that the Motor M1, M2 were connected to the MOTOR_A header on Motor Control Board.
   --- Check the solder joints on the motor  and encoder wires.
   --- Doulbe check the encoder was connedted correctly and to J35 and J32.
   --- Use a multimeter to check continuity (beep test), to ground, to 3V3, to 5V.(use the schematic and board artwork as reference)

Test 36 out of 36
Test: Encoder/Motor Controller B Test
 Right Motor Test       
 Enter "C"ontinue to next instruction,"B"ack to go beginning of last Test, "S"kip to the next Test or "Q"uit

 Instructions:
   - Move the motor wires M1 and M2 to the MOTOR_B header on Motor Control Board.
   - Move encoder wire C1 on to header J33 top pin (closest to inside of board).
   - Move the encoder VCC wire to the middle pin of J33 and encoder ground wire into the bottom (outside) pin J33.
   - Move encoder C2 wire to top pin on J25 (closest to inside of board).
   - then enter "C" 
   - The Serial Monitor should display the Encode count and the motor should be spinning.       NOTE: Once you are finished with this test you will need to enter "C" to stop the scrolling data.

 Instructions:
   - If test worked enter "C" 
   - If did not work,, unplug the MSEduino from power sources (USB) and 
   --- Check that the Motor M1, M2 were connected to the MOTOR_B header on Motor Control Board.
   --- Check the solder joints on the motor  and encoder wires.
   --- Doulbe check the encoder was connedted correctly and to J33 and 25.
   --- Use a multimeter to check continuity (beep test), to ground, to 3V3, to 5V.(use the schematic and board artwork as reference)
